ORCH = WT + r"\backend\orchestration.py"

with io.open(MAIN, "r", encoding="utf-8", newline="") as f:
    lines = f.readlines()  # keep exact newlines

# 1-based inclusive line ranges to MOVE, in main.py order.
# (start, end) — verified against the current file by sentinel checks below.
BLOCKS = [
    ("_finalize_reingest", 368, 378, "def _finalize_reingest(", "        db.prune_segments(video_id, play_after=play_wm, cand_after=cand_wm)"),
    ("_report_config_for_input", 612, 636, "def _report_config_for_input(", "    return cfg"),
    ("_ingest_remote_segments", 976, 1023, "def _ingest_remote_segments(", "    return n"),
    ("_cloud_available", 1026, 1042, "def _cloud_available(", "    return bool(has_job and out_root)"),
    ("_maybe_dispatch_remote", 1045, 1227, "def _maybe_dispatch_remote(", "    )"),
    ("_execute_processing", 1230, 1476, "def _execute_processing(", "        )"),
    ("_remote_input_size_bytes", 1479, 1497, "def _remote_input_size_bytes(", "        return None"),
    ("_CompileError", 1681, 1688, "class _CompileError(", "        self.detail = detail"),
    ("_resolve_compile", 1691, 1737, "def _resolve_compile(", "    return video, kept, out_name"),
    ("_execute_compile", 1790, 1858, "def _execute_compile(", "    }"),
]

# Sentinel-verify every range before touching anything.
errors = []
for name, s, e, first, last in BLOCKS:
    got_first = lines[s - 1].rstrip("\r\n")
    got_last = lines[e - 1].rstrip("\r\n")
    if not got_first.startswith(first):
        errors.append(f"{name}: line {s} is {got_first!r}, expected startswith {first!r}")
    if got_last != last:
        errors.append(f"{name}: line {e} is {got_last!r}, expected {last!r}")
if errors:
    print("SENTINEL FAILURES:")
    for x in errors:
        print(" ", x)
    sys.exit(1)

HEADER = '''"""Processing / compile orchestration, extracted from ``backend.main`` (P23; re-cut of #390).

The full hash -> validate -> segment -> report pipeline (``_execute_processing``), the
Cloud Run dispatch + ingest path (``_maybe_dispatch_remote`` / ``_ingest_remote_segments``),
and the shared compile resolution + worker handler (``_resolve_compile`` / ``_execute_compile``).
Extracted verbatim from ``backend.main`` to shrink the orchestrator; behavior is unchanged.

SEAM DISCIPLINE (same convention as ``backend.api.job_worker``): anything that must remain
monkeypatchable-on-``backend.main`` or that lives in ``backend.main`` (the transitional
``db_instance``/``global_config`` globals and the ``ProcessRequest`` model) is resolved through
the ``backend.main`` module object at call time (``import backend.main as _main``), never as a
bare local. ``backend.main`` re-exports every name below, so ``monkeypatch.setattr(backend.main,
...)`` and ``import backend.main as m; m._execute_processing(...)`` keep working unchanged.
This module must NOT import ``backend.main`` at module level (main imports us for the re-export).
"""

import csv
import json
import logging
import math
import os
import tempfile
from typing import TYPE_CHECKING, Any, Dict, List, Optional, Tuple
from urllib.parse import quote

from backend import storage
from backend.config import AppConfig, StitchingConfig
from backend.pipeline.segmenters.base import segmenter_registry
from backend.pipeline.stitcher.compiler import VideoCompiler
from backend.pipeline.stitcher.watermark_i18n import localize_watermark
from backend.pipeline.validators.base import registry
from backend.reporting import emit_indexer_report
from backend.results import Failure
from backend.utils.hashing import compute_video_id
from backend.utils.paths import output_base, safe_output_filename

if TYPE_CHECKING:  # runtime import would be circular (main imports us for the re-export)
    from backend.main import ProcessRequest  # noqa: F401

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

'''

def block_text(s, e):
    return "".join(lines[s - 1 : e])

out = [HEADER]
for i, (name, s, e, _f, _l) in enumerate(BLOCKS):
    txt = block_text(s, e)

    if name == "_maybe_dispatch_remote":
        # Delta 1: annotation forward-ref (ProcessRequest lives in backend.main; runtime
        # import would be circular). String annotation only — never evaluated at runtime.
        txt = txt.replace(
            "    req: ProcessRequest,\n",
            '    req: "ProcessRequest",\n',
            1,
        )

    if name == "_execute_processing":
        # Delta 2: the backward-compat shim reads main's transitional module globals and the
        # ProcessRequest model — resolve via the backend.main module object at CALL time
        # (identical semantics to the old module-global lookup, incl. monkeypatchability).
        txt = txt.replace(
            '    """The full hash',
            '    """The full hash',
            1,
        )
        # insert the lazy import right after the docstring closes:
        anchor = '    Raises on unexpected internal errors — the caller records those as a job failure\n    """\n'
        assert anchor in txt, "docstring anchor not found in _execute_processing"
        txt = txt.replace(
            anchor,
            anchor
            + "    import backend.main as _main  # call-time seam: main's globals/model stay patchable\n\n",
            1,
        )
        txt = txt.replace(
            "        if req is None and db is not None and isinstance(db, ProcessRequest):",
            "        if req is None and db is not None and isinstance(db, _main.ProcessRequest):",
            1,
        )
        txt = txt.replace(
            "        elif req is None and isinstance(config, ProcessRequest):",
            "        elif req is None and isinstance(config, _main.ProcessRequest):",
            1,
        )
        txt = txt.replace(
            "    if config is None:\n        config = global_config\n",
            "    if config is None:\n        config = _main.global_config\n",
            1,
        )
        txt = txt.replace(
            "    if db is None:\n        db = db_instance\n",
            "    if db is None:\n        db = _main.db_instance\n",
            1,
        )

    out.append(txt)
    if i < len(BLOCKS) - 1:
        out.append("\n\n")

orch_src = "".join(out)
if not orch_src.endswith("\n"):
    orch_src += "\n"
with io.open(ORCH, "w", encoding="utf-8", newline="") as f:
    f.write(orch_src)

# ---- rewrite main.py: remove blocks (bottom-up), insert re-export ----
# Removal ranges include the trailing blank separators BETWEEN moved defs where both
# neighbours moved; where a stayed-def follows, keep exactly the two blank lines.
# We remove exactly the def lines; blank-line normalization below.
to_remove = sorted([(s, e) for (_n, s, e, _f, _l) in BLOCKS], reverse=True)
new_lines = lines[:]
for s, e in to_remove:
    # extend the removal over the following blank lines (they separated this def
    # from the next block); we re-normalize spacing after removal.
    end = e
    while end < len(new_lines) and new_lines[end].strip() == "":
        end += 1
    del new_lines[s - 1 : end]

body = "".join(new_lines)

# normalize: no 3+ consecutive blank lines left behind
while "\n\n\n\n" in body:
    body = body.replace("\n\n\n\n", "\n\n\n")

REEXPORT = '''
# The processing/compile ORCHESTRATION (hash→validate→segment→report pipeline, Cloud Run
# dispatch+ingest, compile resolution + worker handler) now lives in backend.orchestration
# (P23 god-module split; re-cut of #390). Re-exported here so the names stay addressable on
# backend.main — `import backend.main as m; m._execute_processing(...)`, the job worker's
# call-time `_main._execute_compile(...)` seam, and monkeypatch.setattr(backend.main, ...)
# all keep working unchanged.
from backend.orchestration import (  # noqa: F401  (re-exports: names stay addressable on backend.main)
    _cloud_available,
    _CompileError,
    _execute_compile,
    _execute_processing,
    _finalize_reingest,
    _ingest_remote_segments,
    _maybe_dispatch_remote,
    _remote_input_size_bytes,
    _report_config_for_input,
    _resolve_compile,
)
'''

# Insert AFTER the existing job_worker re-export block (keeps the transitional-shim
# commentary together). Anchor on its closing paren + the known following line.
anchor = ")\n\n\ndef _cors_origins_from_env"
# safer: find the job_worker import block end
jw = body.find("from backend.api.job_worker import (")
assert jw != -1, "job_worker re-export block not found"
close = body.find(")\n", jw)
assert close != -1
insert_at = close + 2
body = body[:insert_at] + REEXPORT + body[insert_at:]

with io.open(MAIN, "w", encoding="utf-8", newline="") as f:
    f.write(body)

print("extraction complete.")
print("orchestration.py lines:", orch_src.count("\n"))
print("main.py lines:", body.count("\n"))

### 262. assistant (2026-07-02T09:12:58.076Z)

"""AST-identity verifier for the P23 orchestration extraction.

1. MOVED members: for each of the 10 moved defs/classes, the body in backend/orchestration.py
   must be AST-identical to master's backend/main.py after normalizing ONLY the enumerated
   mechanical deltas (call-time `_main.X` seams -> bare `X`; string annotation -> Name; the
   inserted `import backend.main as _main` line dropped).
2. RETAINED members: every top-level def/class still in backend/main.py must be AST-identical
   to its master version (the file may differ in imports/re-exports/comments; function BODIES
   must not have changed).
3. NAME RESOLUTION: orchestration.py must contain no unresolvable top-level names
   (catches a missed import).

Exit 0 = all green. Prints a delta report.
"""

import ast
import subprocess
import sys

WT = r"C:\Users\avidu\AppData\Local\Temp\claude\C--Users-avidu-Projects-badminton-highlight-indexer\accd47af-95a3-46a7-9b51-b3eb5066a777\scratchpad\wt-docs"

MOVED = [
    "_finalize_reingest",
    "_report_config_for_input",
    "_ingest_remote_segments",
    "_cloud_available",
    "_maybe_dispatch_remote",
    "_execute_processing",
    "_remote_input_size_bytes",
    "_CompileError",
    "_resolve_compile",
    "_execute_compile",
]

master_main_src = subprocess.run(
    ["git", "-C", WT, "show", "origin/master:backend/main.py"],
    capture_output=True, text=True, encoding="utf-8",
).stdout
new_main_src = open(WT + r"\backend\main.py", encoding="utf-8").read()
orch_src = open(WT + r"\backend\orchestration.py", encoding="utf-8").read()

master_main = ast.parse(master_main_src)
new_main = ast.parse(new_main_src)
orch = ast.parse(orch_src)


def top_defs(tree):
    return {
        n.name: n
        for n in tree.body
        if isinstance(n, (ast.FunctionDef, ast.AsyncFunctionDef, ast.ClassDef))
    }


class Normalize(ast.NodeTransformer):
    """Rewrite the enumerated deltas in the NEW orchestration AST back to master's shape."""

    def visit_Attribute(self, node):
        self.generic_visit(node)
        # _main.X  ->  X   (call-time seam through the backend.main module object)
        if isinstance(node.value, ast.Name) and node.value.id == "_main":
            return ast.copy_location(ast.Name(id=node.attr, ctx=node.ctx), node)
        return node

    def visit_Import(self, node):
        # drop the inserted `import backend.main as _main`
        names = [a for a in node.names if not (a.name == "backend.main" and a.asname == "_main")]
        if not names:
            return None
        node.names = names
        return node

    def visit_arg(self, node):
        self.generic_visit(node)
        # string annotation "ProcessRequest" -> Name ProcessRequest
        if (
            isinstance(node.annotation, ast.Constant)
            and isinstance(node.annotation.value, str)
            and node.annotation.value == "ProcessRequest"
        ):
            node.annotation = ast.copy_location(
                ast.Name(id="ProcessRequest", ctx=ast.Load()), node.annotation
            )
        return node


def dump(node):
    return ast.dump(node, include_attributes=False)


fails = []
report = []

master_defs = top_defs(master_main)
orch_defs = top_defs(orch)
new_defs = top_defs(new_main)

# 1. moved members
for name in MOVED:
    if name not in master_defs:
        fails.append(f"moved {name}: not found in master main.py")
        continue
    if name not in orch_defs:
        fails.append(f"moved {name}: not found in orchestration.py")
        continue
    if name in new_defs:
        fails.append(f"moved {name}: STILL DEFINED in new main.py (should be re-export only)")
        continue
    normalized = Normalize().visit(ast.parse(ast.unparse(orch_defs[name])))
    ast.fix_missing_locations(normalized)
    a = dump(ast.parse(ast.unparse(master_defs[name])).body[0])
    b = dump(normalized.body[0])
    if a == b:
        report.append(f"  MOVED {name}: AST-identical (after normalizing enumerated deltas)")
    else:
        fails.append(f"moved {name}: AST DIFFERS beyond the enumerated deltas")
        # locate first difference for debugging
        for i, (ca, cb) in enumerate(zip(a, b)):
            if ca != cb:
                fails.append(f"    first divergence at char {i}: ...{a[max(0,i-60):i+60]!r} vs ...{b[max(0,i-60):i+60]!r}")
                break

# 2. retained members
retained_checked = 0
for name, node in new_defs.items():
    if name in MOVED:
        continue
    if name not in master_defs:
        fails.append(f"retained {name}: NEW top-level def not in master (unexpected in a pure move)")
        continue
    a = dump(ast.parse(ast.unparse(master_defs[name])).body[0])
    b = dump(ast.parse(ast.unparse(node)).body[0])
    if a != b:
        fails.append(f"retained {name}: BODY CHANGED vs master")
    else:
        retained_checked += 1
# ensure nothing besides the moved set disappeared from main
for name in master_defs:
    if name not in MOVED and name not in new_defs:
        fails.append(f"retained {name}: DISAPPEARED from main.py but not in the moved set")

report.append(f"  RETAINED: {retained_checked} top-level defs/classes AST-identical to master")

# 3. name resolution in orchestration.py
import builtins
known = set(dir(builtins))
for n in ast.walk(orch):
    if isinstance(n, (ast.Import,)):
        for a in n.names:
            known.add((a.asname or a.name).split(".")[0])
    elif isinstance(n, ast.ImportFrom):
        for a in n.names:
            known.add(a.asname or a.name)
    elif isinstance(n, (ast.FunctionDef, ast.AsyncFunctionDef, ast.ClassDef)):
        known.add(n.name)
    elif isinstance(n, ast.Assign):
        for t in n.targets:
            if isinstance(t, ast.Name):
                known.add(t.id)


class NameCheck(ast.NodeVisitor):
    def __init__(self):
        self.unresolved = set()
        self.local_stacks = []

    def visit_FunctionDef(self, node):
        locs = {a.arg for a in node.args.args + node.args.kwonlyargs}
        if node.args.vararg: locs.add(node.args.vararg.arg)
        if node.args.kwarg: locs.add(node.args.kwarg.arg)
        for sub in ast.walk(node):
            if isinstance(sub, ast.Name) and isinstance(sub.ctx, ast.Store):
                locs.add(sub.id)
            elif isinstance(sub, (ast.Import,)):
                for a in sub.names: locs.add((a.asname or a.name).split(".")[0])
            elif isinstance(sub, ast.ImportFrom):
                for a in sub.names: locs.add(a.asname or a.name)
            elif isinstance(sub, (ast.FunctionDef, ast.AsyncFunctionDef, ast.ClassDef)) and sub is not node:
                locs.add(sub.name)
            elif isinstance(sub, ast.ExceptHandler) and sub.name:
                locs.add(sub.name)
            elif isinstance(sub, (ast.comprehension,)):
                for t in ast.walk(sub.target):
                    if isinstance(t, ast.Name): locs.add(t.id)
            elif isinstance(sub, (ast.With, ast.AsyncWith)):
                for item in sub.items:
                    if item.optional_vars:
                        for t in ast.walk(item.optional_vars):
                            if isinstance(t, ast.Name): locs.add(t.id)
        for sub in ast.walk(node):
            if isinstance(sub, ast.Name) and isinstance(sub.ctx, ast.Load):
                if sub.id not in locs and sub.id not in known:
                    self.unresolved.add(f"{node.name}: {sub.id}")

    visit_AsyncFunctionDef = visit_FunctionDef


nc = NameCheck()
for n in orch.body:
    if isinstance(n, (ast.FunctionDef, ast.AsyncFunctionDef)):
        nc.visit(n)
    elif isinstance(n, ast.ClassDef):
        for m in n.body:
            if isinstance(m, (ast.FunctionDef, ast.AsyncFunctionDef)):
                nc.visit(m)
if nc.unresolved:
    for u in sorted(nc.unresolved):
        fails.append(f"orchestration.py unresolved name: {u}")
else:
    report.append("  NAMES: all names in orchestration.py resolve (imports/locals/builtins)")

print("\n".join(report))
if fails:
    print("\nFAILURES:")
    for f in fails:
        print(" ", f)
    sys.exit(1)
print("\nAST-IDENTITY: ALL GREEN")

### 283. assistant (2026-07-02T09:18:09.383Z)

"""Anti-bloat ratchet for the ``backend.main`` god-module split (P23; re-cut of #390).

``backend/main.py`` grew 1448 -> 2360 lines in under a week because every endpoint/fix PR
landed in it. The P23 extraction moved the processing/compile orchestration to
``backend/orchestration.py``; this ratchet keeps the split from silently regressing:

- **Line budget:** ``backend/main.py`` must stay at/below ``MAIN_LINE_BUDGET``. When a
  later split lands (CLI -> P23 step 2, routes -> P24), TIGHTEN the budget to the new
  size + small slack — never loosen it without an owner-visible PR discussion.
- **Route ceiling:** no NEW ``@app.<method>`` routes in ``main.py`` — new endpoints go in
  ``backend/api/`` (see ``backend/api/uploads.py`` for the pattern).
- **Re-export contract:** every orchestration name stays addressable on ``backend.main``
  (``import backend.main as m; m._execute_processing`` and
  ``monkeypatch.setattr(backend.main, ...)`` are load-bearing seams — see
  ``backend/api/job_worker.py``'s module docstring).

If this test fails your PR: extract, don't inline. Umbrella tracker:
``docs/AUDIT_REPORT_khelsutra-guru_2026-07-02.md`` (Remediation tracker P23/P24).
"""

import ast
from pathlib import Path

REPO = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
MAIN = REPO / "backend" / "main.py"

# main.py was 1703 lines right after the P23 orchestration extraction (2026-07-02).
# Slack covers small in-place fixes; systematic growth must go to a new module instead.
MAIN_LINE_BUDGET = 1750

# The 18 routes present at the P23 extraction. New endpoints belong in backend/api/.
MAIN_ROUTE_BUDGET = 18

ORCHESTRATION_REEXPORTS = [
    "_cloud_available",
    "_CompileError",
    "_execute_compile",
    "_execute_processing",
    "_finalize_reingest",
    "_ingest_remote_segments",
    "_maybe_dispatch_remote",
    "_remote_input_size_bytes",
    "_report_config_for_input",
    "_resolve_compile",
]


def test_main_py_line_budget():
    n = sum(1 for _ in MAIN.open(encoding="utf-8"))
    assert n <= MAIN_LINE_BUDGET, (
        f"backend/main.py is {n} lines (budget {MAIN_LINE_BUDGET}). Don't grow the "
        f"god-module back — extract to backend/orchestration.py / backend/api/ / a new "
        f"module instead (P23/P24 in docs/AUDIT_REPORT_khelsutra-guru_2026-07-02.md). "
        f"If a later split shrank main.py, TIGHTEN this budget in the same PR."
    )


def _count_app_routes(tree: ast.Module) -> int:
    n = 0
    for node in ast.walk(tree):
        if not isinstance(node, (ast.FunctionDef, ast.AsyncFunctionDef)):
            continue
        for dec in node.decorator_list:
            call = dec if isinstance(dec, ast.Call) else None
            target = call.func if call else dec
            if (
                isinstance(target, ast.Attribute)
                and isinstance(target.value, ast.Name)
                and target.value.id == "app"
            ):
                n += 1
                break
    return n


def test_main_py_route_ceiling():
    tree = ast.parse(MAIN.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    n = _count_app_routes(tree)
    assert n <= MAIN_ROUTE_BUDGET, (
        f"backend/main.py defines {n} @app routes (ceiling {MAIN_ROUTE_BUDGET}). "
        f"New endpoints go in backend/api/ (see backend/api/uploads.py), not main.py."
    )


def test_orchestration_names_stay_addressable_on_main():
    """The re-export seam is load-bearing: the job worker resolves `_main._execute_compile`
    at call time and tests monkeypatch these names on backend.main. They must stay the
    SAME objects on both modules (a re-export, not a copy/fork)."""
    import backend.main as m
    import backend.orchestration as o

    for name in ORCHESTRATION_REEXPORTS:
        assert getattr(m, name) is getattr(o, name), (
            f"backend.main.{name} is not the backend.orchestration.{name} object — "
            f"the re-export seam (monkeypatchability on backend.main) is broken."
        )

def test_cloud_video_id_divergence_is_tolerated_with_warning(cloud_env, caplog):
    """If the worker re-hashes different bytes (result.video_id != local id), ingest still proceeds
    under the LOCAL id but logs a WARNING so the orphaned-report condition is diagnosable."""
    db, _bucket, monkeypatch = cloud_env
    _inject_fake(monkeypatch, _FakeCloudClient(video_id="DIVERGED"))
    with caplog.at_level("WARNING", logger="backend.main"):
        out = m._execute_processing(
            m.ProcessRequest(video_path="gs://b/clip.mp4", segmenter="wasb_hybrid")
        )
    assert out["status"] == "success"
    assert len(db.query_play_segments(out["video_id"], {})) == 2
    assert any(
        "DIVERGED" in r.getMessage() and "!= local" in r.getMessage()
        for r in caplog.records
    )

def test_unknown_compute_value_falls_back_to_server_default(cloud_env, caplog):
    """An unrecognised compute value is ignored (logged) and the server default applies — here a
    cloud_run server still dispatches to the cloud."""
    db, _bucket, monkeypatch = cloud_env
    fake = _FakeCloudClient()
    _inject_fake(monkeypatch, fake)
    with caplog.at_level("WARNING", logger="backend.main"):
        out = m._execute_processing(
            m.global_config,
            m.db_instance,
            m.ProcessRequest(
                video_path="gs://b/clip.mp4", segmenter="wasb_hybrid", compute="banana"
            ),
        )
    assert out["status"] == "success"
    assert len(fake.calls) == 1  # fell back to the configured cloud_run default
    assert any("unknown compute" in r.getMessage() for r in caplog.records)

### 319. assistant (2026-07-02T09:38:57.449Z)

## Summary

**Step 1 of the `main.py` god-module split** (umbrella Remediation-tracker **P23**; owner-approved re-cut of **#390**, which was closed — its extracted copies predated the #417/#424–#427 rewrites of the very functions it moved). Extracts the **processing + compile orchestration** into a new `backend/orchestration.py`, taking `main.py` from **2360 → 1703 lines (−28%)**, and adds an **anti-bloat ratchet** so the god-module cannot silently regrow.

## What moved → `backend/orchestration.py` (10 members, verbatim)

| Member | Purpose |
|---|---|
| `_finalize_reingest` | destroy-AFTER-confirm segment commit |
| `_report_config_for_input` | keeps remote-input reports out of disposable scratch (#427) |
| `_ingest_remote_segments` | cloud job `intervals.csv` → DB rows |
| `_cloud_available` | can this box dispatch a cloud job? |
| `_maybe_dispatch_remote` | Cloud Run dispatch + ingest (default-OFF; incl. #417 salvage path) |
| `_execute_processing` | full hash → validate → segment → report pipeline (incl. #424 idempotency) |
| `_remote_input_size_bytes` | best-effort remote stat for the free-space guard |
| `_CompileError` / `_resolve_compile` | shared submit/worker compile resolution |
| `_execute_compile` | worker handler for a compile job (incl. #426/#427 behavior) |

`backend.main` **re-exports all 10 names** — same convention as the `backend.api.job_worker` extraction — so `import backend.main as m; m._execute_processing(...)`, the worker's call-time `_main._execute_compile(...)` seam, and every `monkeypatch.setattr(backend.main, ...)` in the suite keep working unchanged.

## Proof this is behavior-preserving

Machine-verified (scripted AST comparison vs `origin/master`, run on the final commit state):
- ✅ All **10 moved members are AST-identical** to master after normalizing the **7 enumerated mechanical deltas** below.
- ✅ All **51 retained** top-level defs/classes in `main.py` are **AST-identical to master** (only imports/re-exports changed around them).
- ✅ Name-resolution scan: no unresolved names in the new module.

### The 7 enumerated deltas (all seam mechanics; zero logic change)
Call-time `_main.<name>` resolution is *semantically identical* to master (master's bare names resolved through `backend.main`'s module dict at call time; `_main.<name>` reads the same dict) — it exists so the names tests **rebind on `backend.main`** still intercept the moved code:
1. `_execute_processing`: inserted `import backend.main as _main` (call-time seam import).
2. `_execute_processing`: `global_config` / `db_instance` fallbacks → `_main.global_config` / `_main.db_instance`.
3. `_execute_processing`: the two `isinstance(..., ProcessRequest)` shim checks → `_main.ProcessRequest`.
4. `_execute_processing`: `compute_video_id` → `_main.compute_video_id` (tests rebind it: `test_api_endpoints.py:817`).
5. `_execute_processing`: `emit_indexer_report` → `_main.emit_indexer_report` (rebound at `:782`).
6. `_execute_compile`: inserted `import backend.main as _main`; `VideoCompiler` → `_main.VideoCompiler` (rebound at `:411,455,486,531,575`).
7. `_maybe_dispatch_remote`: `req: ProcessRequest` → string forward-ref (runtime import would be circular; `TYPE_CHECKING` import for mypy).

Plus two `# type: ignore[...]` comments (AST-invisible) — which let `backend.orchestration` stay **out of the mypy quarantine**: ~657 lines leave the quarantined `backend.main` surface and are now type-checked (a P24 ratchet down-payment).

## Anti-bloat ratchet (new `tests/test_main_module_budget.py`)

`main.py` grew 1448 → 2360 in a week because every endpoint PR landed in it. Now CI-enforced:
- **Line budget 1750** — later splits must TIGHTEN it in the same PR; growth past it fails with a message pointing to the extraction convention.
- **Route ceiling 18** — new endpoints go in `backend/api/` (pattern: `backend/api/uploads.py`), not `main.py`.
- **Re-export contract** — the 10 names must remain the *same objects* on `backend.main` and `backend.orchestration` (the monkeypatch seam is load-bearing).

## Verification

- Full suite: **1579 passed, 13 skipped**; coverage **85.16%** vs floor 70 (no regression).
- `ruff check` + `ruff format --check`: clean. `mypy backend training --no-incremental`: **clean, 142 files** (with `google-cloud-storage` installed).
- AST-identity report: **ALL GREEN** on the exact commit state.
- **Adversarial 3-lens review** (seams / imports / behavior) before commit: seam fidelity **empirically confirmed** — every `monkeypatch.setattr(backend.main, ...)` target, the job-worker call-time seams, retained-route call sites, and direct `m.<name>` callers verified at cited lines; 162 targeted tests green. The review also *caught* the initial version's missing seam routing for `VideoCompiler`/`emit_indexer_report`/`compute_video_id` (6 test failures) — fixed via deltas 4–6 and re-verified. Circular imports, the `python -m backend.main` double-module trap, packaging, and removed-import safety verified clean. The logger-name change for moved code (`backend.main` → `backend.orchestration`) was checked against every caplog assertion — none assert on logger names; suite green.
- Umbrella tracker P23 row updated in a follow-up commit on this branch.

## Follow-ups (tracked, NOT in this PR)
- **P23 step 2 / P24:** extract the CLI (`main()`, ~500 lines) and continue route/helper moves; tighten the ratchet budget each time; lift remaining quarantine.

199	def _open_browser_when_ready(url: str, health_url: str, timeout: float = 60.0) -> bool:
200	    """Poll `health_url` until the server is up, then open `url` in the user's default browser.
201	    Returns True if a browser open was attempted. No-op (returns False) when RALLY_NO_BROWSER is set
202	    (tests / headless) or the server never becomes healthy (don't open a dead page). Never raises."""
203	    if os.environ.get("RALLY_NO_BROWSER"):
204	        return False
205	    import time as _time
206	    import urllib.request
207	
208	    deadline = _time.monotonic() + timeout
209	    while _time.monotonic() < deadline:
210	        try:
211	            with urllib.request.urlopen(health_url, timeout=2) as r:
212	                if getattr(r, "status", 200) == 200:
213	                    break
214	        except Exception:
215	            _time.sleep(0.3)
216	    else:
217	        return False
218	    try:
219	        webbrowser.open(url)
220	    except Exception:
221	        pass
222	    return True
223	
224	
225	# Serves static frontend files directly (now under api/ per approved structure).
226	# PACKAGING_PLAN.md P0a: resolve PACKAGE-relative (not CWD-relative) so launching from an
227	# arbitrary directory finds the shipped assets instead of creating a stray ./backend/api/static.
228	# (The legacy in-engine dashboard; the real SPA gets bundled here in P1a.)
229	_STATIC_DIR = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)), "api", "static")
230	try:
231	    os.makedirs(_STATIC_DIR, exist_ok=True)
232	except OSError:
233	    # The dir ships with the package (a no-op create); guard against a read-only install
234	    # tree (system-installed Python) since exist_ok suppresses FileExistsError, not PermissionError.
235	    pass
236	app.mount("/static", StaticFiles(directory=_STATIC_DIR), name="static")
237	
238	# P1b: serve the bundled KhelSutra SPA single-origin. `backend/ui/` is the committed snapshot
239	# (produced by scripts/bundle_ui.sh); fall back to the legacy in-engine dashboard only if it's
240	# absent (a source checkout with no vendored UI). The SPA calls same-origin `/api`, so no SPA change
241	# is needed; it is query-param driven (?video=…), so serving index.html at `/` + /assets is enough
242	# (no client-side path routing → no history-fallback catch-all, which would also be a route-order hazard).
243	_BUNDLED_UI_DIR = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__)), "ui")
244	_UI_DIR = (
245	    _BUNDLED_UI_DIR
246	    if os.path.isfile(os.path.join(_BUNDLED_UI_DIR, "index.html"))
247	    else _STATIC_DIR

105	def _cors_origins_from_env(env: Optional[Dict[str, str]] = None) -> List[str]:
106	    """M9: CORS allow-origins from the ``RALLY_CORS_ALLOW_ORIGINS`` env var (comma-separated),
107	    default ``["*"]``. Read from the ENV (not a cwd ``config.json``) so importing ``backend.main``
108	    does zero filesystem I/O — a hosted/multi-tenant deploy sets this to lock CORS to its origin."""
109	    src = os.environ if env is None else env
110	    raw = (src.get("RALLY_CORS_ALLOW_ORIGINS", "") or "").strip()
111	    origins = [o.strip() for o in raw.split(",") if o.strip()]
112	    return origins or ["*"]
113	
114	
115	# CORS for the web UI. allow_origins='*' with allow_credentials=True is rejected by browsers per the
116	# fetch spec + is unsafe to carry into multi-tenant; this tool uses no cookies, so credentials stay
117	# off. The origin list is configurable via the env var above (default ["*"]) so a hosted deploy can
118	# lock it to its origin; the middleware is fixed at startup.
119	app.add_middleware(
120	    CORSMiddleware,
121	    allow_origins=_cors_origins_from_env(),
122	    allow_credentials=False,
123	    allow_methods=["*"],
124	    allow_headers=["*"],
125	)
126	
127	
128	def _allowed_hosts_from_env(env: Optional[Dict[str, str]] = None) -> List[str]:
129	    """P0c: Host-header allowlist — a DNS-rebinding guard for the localhost appliance. A malicious
130	    web page can point a domain it controls at 127.0.0.1 and script requests to the local server;
131	    the browser sends ``Host: attacker.com``, which this rejects (the server only answers loopback
132	    Host headers by default). ``RALLY_ALLOWED_HOSTS`` (comma-separated) overrides; the default keeps
133	    Starlette's TestClient host ``testserver`` so the in-process suite isn't broken. Read from the
134	    ENV (not config.json) so importing ``backend.main`` does zero filesystem I/O; a routable/edge
135	    deploy sets its public host (or ``*`` when fronted by an auth gate that validates the host)."""
136	    src = os.environ if env is None else env
137	    raw = (src.get("RALLY_ALLOWED_HOSTS", "") or "").strip()
138	    hosts = [h.strip() for h in raw.split(",") if h.strip()]
139	    # IPv4 loopback (the default bind) + the TestClient host. NB: Starlette's TrustedHostMiddleware
140	    # matches on host.split(":")[0], which mangles a bracketed IPv6 Host ("[::1]:port"), so IPv6
141	    # loopback is NOT supported by this guard — access the appliance via 127.0.0.1/localhost.
142	    return hosts or ["localhost", "127.0.0.1", "testserver"]
143	
144	
145	# DNS-rebinding guard. Bound to localhost by default, so only loopback Host headers are honoured
146	# (a remote page can't read responses cross-origin AND its Host header is rejected here). www_redirect
147	# off so an unexpected ``www.`` host is rejected rather than 307-redirected.
148	app.add_middleware(
149	    TrustedHostMiddleware,
150	    allowed_hosts=_allowed_hosts_from_env(),
151	    www_redirect=False,
152	)
153	
154	
155	def _resolve_bind_host(
156	    config: Optional[AppConfig] = None, cli_host: Optional[str] = None
157	) -> str:
158	    """P0c: the server bind address. Precedence: ``--host`` > ``RALLY_BIND_HOST`` env > ``0.0.0.0``
159	    when an edge-auth profile is active (``security.edge_auth_enabled`` — i.e. there IS an auth gate
160	    in front) > ``127.0.0.1`` (default: localhost-only, today's behaviour). Binding a routable
161	    interface with no auth gate is unsafe, hence the localhost default; widen it consciously."""
162	    if cli_host and cli_host.strip():
163	        return cli_host.strip()
164	    env = os.environ.get("RALLY_BIND_HOST")
165	    if env and env.strip():
166	        return env.strip()
167	    sec = getattr(config, "security", None)
168	    if getattr(sec, "edge_auth_enabled", False):
169	        return "0.0.0.0"  # behind a Cloudflare-Access-style edge gate (M9) — bind all interfaces
170	    return "127.0.0.1"
171	
172	
173	# --- P2: the friendly `indexer --app` launcher (cross-platform, no native component — O1) ---
174	
175	
176	def _port_is_free(host: str, port: int) -> bool:
177	    """True if (host, port) can be bound right now (best-effort; a TOCTOU race is acceptable —
178	    uvicorn surfaces a real bind failure loudly)."""
179	    with socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM) as s:
180	        try:
181	            s.bind((host, port))
182	            return True
183	        except OSError:
184	            return False
185	
186	
187	def _find_free_port(host: str) -> int:
188	    """An OS-assigned free port on host."""
189	    with socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_STREAM) as s:
190	        s.bind((host, 0))
191	        return int(s.getsockname()[1])
192	
193	
194	def _resolve_app_port(host: str, port: int) -> int:
195	    """For --app: keep `port` if free, else fall back to a free one so a busy 8000 never blocks the app."""
196	    return port if _port_is_free(host, port) else _find_free_port(host)
197

    ("_allowed_hosts_from_env", 128, 142, "def _allowed_hosts_from_env(", '    return hosts or ["localhost", "127.0.0.1", "testserver"]'),

    ("_open_browser_when_ready", 199, 222, "def _open_browser_when_ready(", "    return True"),
]

errors = []
for name, s, e, first, last in BLOCKS:
    gf = lines[s - 1].rstrip("\r\n")
    gl = lines[e - 1].rstrip("\r\n")
    if not gf.startswith(first):
        errors.append(f"{name}: line {s} is {gf!r}, expected startswith {first!r}")
    if gl != last:
        errors.append(f"{name}: line {e} is {gl!r}, expected {last!r}")

    for x in errors:
        print(" ", x)
    sys.exit(1)

HEADER = '''"""Server / network bootstrap helpers, extracted from ``backend.main`` (P24 god-module split).

Pure, side-effect-free helpers for the CORS/host-allowlist middleware config and the friendly
``indexer --app`` launcher (bind-host precedence, free-port fallback, open-browser-when-ready).
No module globals, no ``app`` object, no ``backend.main`` import — a plain move; ``backend.main``
re-imports these names (ABOVE its ``app = FastAPI(...)`` so the module-level ``add_middleware``
calls still resolve them) and re-exports them so ``import backend.main as m; m._resolve_bind_host``
and the like keep working unchanged.
"""

import os
import socket
import webbrowser
from typing import Dict, List, Optional

from backend.config import AppConfig

'''

out = [HEADER]
for i, (name, s, e, _f, _l) in enumerate(BLOCKS):
    out.append("".join(lines[s - 1 : e]))
    if i < len(BLOCKS) - 1:
        out.append("\n\n")
dst_src = "".join(out)
if not dst_src.endswith("\n"):
    dst_src += "\n"
with io.open(DST, "w", encoding="utf-8", newline="\n") as f:
    f.write(dst_src)

# remove blocks bottom-up (+ trailing blank lines), then normalize spacing
new_lines = lines[:]
for s, e in sorted([(b[1], b[2]) for b in BLOCKS], reverse=True):
    end = e
    while end < len(new_lines) and new_lines[end].strip() == "":
        end += 1
    del new_lines[s - 1 : end]
body = "".join(new_lines)
while "\n\n\n\n" in body:
    body = body.replace("\n\n\n\n", "\n\n\n")

REIMPORT = '''# Server/network bootstrap helpers (CORS/host-allowlist config + the `indexer --app` launcher)
# live in backend.server_runtime (P24 god-module split). Imported HERE, above `app = FastAPI(...)`,
# because the module-level add_middleware(...) below calls _cors_origins_from_env/_allowed_hosts_from_env
# at import time; re-exported so `import backend.main as m; m._resolve_bind_host(...)` keeps working.

    _port_is_free,
    _resolve_app_port,

body = body[:insert_at] + REIMPORT + body[insert_at:]

"""AST-identity verifier for the P24 server_runtime extraction (a PURE move — no deltas)."""

import ast
import subprocess
import sys

WT = r"C:\Users\avidu\AppData\Local\Temp\claude\C--Users-avidu-Projects-badminton-highlight-indexer\accd47af-95a3-46a7-9b51-b3eb5066a777\scratchpad\wt-docs"
MOVED = [
    "_cors_origins_from_env", "_allowed_hosts_from_env", "_resolve_bind_host",
    "_port_is_free", "_find_free_port", "_resolve_app_port", "_open_browser_when_ready",
]

master = ast.parse(subprocess.run(
    ["git", "-C", WT, "show", "origin/master:backend/main.py"],
    capture_output=True, text=True, encoding="utf-8").stdout)
new_main = ast.parse(open(WT + r"\backend\main.py", encoding="utf-8").read())
dst = ast.parse(open(WT + r"\backend\server_runtime.py", encoding="utf-8").read())


def defs(t):
    return {n.name: n for n in t.body if isinstance(n, (ast.FunctionDef, ast.AsyncFunctionDef, ast.ClassDef))}


md, nd, dd = defs(master), defs(new_main), defs(dst)
fails, report = [], []

for name in MOVED:
    if name not in dd:
        fails.append(f"moved {name}: missing from server_runtime.py"); continue
    if name in nd:
        fails.append(f"moved {name}: STILL defined in new main.py"); continue
    a = ast.dump(ast.parse(ast.unparse(md[name])).body[0])
    b = ast.dump(ast.parse(ast.unparse(dd[name])).body[0])
    (report if a == b else fails).append(
        f"  MOVED {name}: AST-identical" if a == b else f"moved {name}: AST DIFFERS (pure move expected identity)")

checked = 0
for name, node in nd.items():
    if name in MOVED:
        continue
    if name not in md:
        fails.append(f"retained {name}: not in master (unexpected)"); continue
    if ast.dump(ast.parse(ast.unparse(md[name])).body[0]) != ast.dump(ast.parse(ast.unparse(node)).body[0]):
        fails.append(f"retained {name}: BODY CHANGED vs master")
    else:
        checked += 1
for name in md:
    if name not in MOVED and name not in nd:
        fails.append(f"retained {name}: DISAPPEARED from main.py but not in moved set")
report.append(f"  RETAINED: {checked} defs/classes AST-identical to master")

print("\n".join(report))
if fails:
    print("\nFAILURES:"); [print(" ", f) for f in fails]; sys.exit(1)
print("\nAST-IDENTITY: ALL GREEN")

  backend.main import.

### 405. assistant (2026-07-02T09:56:51.259Z)

## Summary

**P23 step 2** of the `main.py` god-module split (follows #431). Moves the **pure server/network bootstrap helpers** into a new `backend/server_runtime.py`: **`main.py` 1703 → 1623 lines**.

Deliberately **does not** touch `main()` / the CLI yet — that block mutates the `global_config` / `db_instance` / `_instance_lock` module globals, is the `indexer` console entry point, and carries the `python -m backend.main` double-module trap; it needs a global-mutation-aware extraction and is the next increment (P24), not this PR.

## What moved → `backend/server_runtime.py` (7 pure helpers)

- `_cors_origins_from_env`, `_allowed_hosts_from_env` — CORS / DNS-rebinding host-allowlist config (read from env; zero FS I/O).
- `_resolve_bind_host` — bind-address precedence (`--host` > env > edge-auth-widen > loopback).
- `_port_is_free`, `_find_free_port`, `_resolve_app_port` — `indexer --app` free-port fallback.
- `_open_browser_when_ready` — poll health → open browser (no-op under `RALLY_NO_BROWSER`).

Pure, stateless, no module globals, no `app`, no `backend.main` import.

## Behavior-preserving proof

- **Machine-verified AST-IDENTICAL** (a *pure* move — no deltas): all 7 moved helpers exactly identical to master; all 44 retained `main.py` defs/classes identical.
- `main.py` re-imports them **above `app = FastAPI(...)`** (the module-level `add_middleware(...)` calls `_cors_origins_from_env` / `_allowed_hosts_from_env` at import time) and **re-exports** them, so `import backend.main as m; m._resolve_bind_host(...)` (24 test call sites) keeps working. Removed the now-unused `socket` / `webbrowser` imports from `main.py`.
- `tests/test_app_launcher.py`: 3 unit tests patched the `webbrowser` **module attribute** the moved function uses (`m.webbrowser`); repointed to `backend.server_runtime` (where the function now lives) — **no production behavior change**. Caught by the full suite pre-commit and fixed.

## Anti-bloat ratchet tightened

`tests/test_main_module_budget.py`: `MAIN_LINE_BUDGET` **1750 → 1650**, and `server_runtime` added to the "where new code goes" convention (server/launcher helpers → `backend/server_runtime.py`). This is the ratchet's designed behavior — each split tightens the ceiling.

## Verification

- Full suite: **1579 passed, 13 skipped**; coverage **85.16%** vs floor 70. `ruff` + `ruff format --check` clean. `mypy backend training --no-incremental` clean. AST-identity report ALL GREEN.
- Note: `test_bind_and_host.py::test_loopback_host_header_is_accepted` has a **pre-existing** test-ordering dependency (needs an earlier `create_app`) — it fails *in isolation on clean master too* and passes in the full suite; unaffected by this change.
- Umbrella tracker P23 row + changelog updated in-branch.

## Roadmap (tracked, NOT in this PR)
- **P24 / next:** the delicate `main()` + CLI extraction (own the `global` writes via a thin `main.py` shim that keeps the globals authoritative; preserve the `python -m` trap), then convert route handlers to an `APIRouter` module. Each behind the same AST-identity + full-suite + review discipline, tightening the ratchet each time.
